					<div id="mw-content-text" class="mw-body-content mw-content-ltr" lang="en" dir="ltr"><div class="mw-parser-output"><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none">Overview of the world's largest nations</div>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1033289096">.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+link+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}</style><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">"List of largest countries" redirects here. For largest countries by population, see <a href="/wiki/List_of_countries_and_dependencies_by_population" title="List of countries and dependencies by population">List of countries and dependencies by population</a>.</div>
<p class="mw-empty-elt">

</p>
<div class="thumb tright"><div class="thumbinner" style="width:302px;"><a href="/wiki/File:Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg" class="image"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7f/Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg/300px-Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg.png" decoding="async" width="300" height="200" class="thumbimage" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7f/Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg/450px-Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/7/7f/Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg/600px-Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="341" /></a>  <div class="thumbcaption"><div class="magnify"><a href="/wiki/File:Fuller_projection_with_largest_countries.svg" class="internal" title="Enlarge"></a></div><a href="/wiki/Dymaxion_map" title="Dymaxion map">Dymaxion map</a> of the world with the 30 largest countries and territories by total area according to the table below, <span class="nowrap">roughly to scale</span>.</div></div></div>

</div>
<div class="thumbcaption">
<p>The top 10 largest countries occupy nearly half of the world's dry land.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (March 2023)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup>
</p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r981673959">.mw-parser-output .legend{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid-column}.mw-parser-output .legend-color{display:inline-block;min-width:1.25em;height:1.25em;line-height:1.25;margin:1px 0;text-align:center;border:1px solid black;background-color:transparent;color:black}.mw-parser-output .legend-text{}</style><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#1F78B4; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Russia" title="Russia">Russia</a> (11%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#33A02C; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/China" title="China">China</a> (6.3%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#E31A1C; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/United_States" title="United States">United States</a> (6.1%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#FF7F00; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Canada" title="Canada">Canada</a><sup id="cite_ref-Canada_1-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Canada-1">&#91;Note 1&#93;</a></sup> (6.1%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#6A3D9A; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Brazil" title="Brazil">Brazil</a> (5.6%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#B15928; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Australia" title="Australia">Australia</a> (5.2%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#A6CEE3; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/India" title="India">India</a> (2.0%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#B2DF8A; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Argentina" title="Argentina">Argentina</a> (1.8%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#FB9A99; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Kazakhstan" title="Kazakhstan">Kazakhstan</a> (1.8%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#FDBF6F; color:;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Algeria" title="Algeria">Algeria</a> (1.6%)</div><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959"/><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:white; color:black;">&#160;</span>&#160;Other (52.5%)</div>
</div>
</div></div>
<p>This is a list of the world's countries and their dependencies by land, water, and total area, ranked by total area.
</p><p>Entries in this list include, but are not limited to, those in the <a href="/wiki/ISO_3166-1" title="ISO 3166-1">ISO 3166-1</a> standard, which includes <a href="/wiki/Sovereign_state" title="Sovereign state">sovereign states</a> and <a href="/wiki/Dependent_territory" title="Dependent territory">dependent territories</a>. All 193 <a href="/wiki/Member_states_of_the_United_Nations" title="Member states of the United Nations">member states of the United Nations</a> plus the two observer states are given a rank number. <a href="/wiki/List_of_states_with_limited_recognition" title="List of states with limited recognition">Largely unrecognised states</a> not in ISO 3166-1 are included in the list in ranked order. The areas of such largely unrecognised <a href="/wiki/State_(polity)" title="State (polity)">states</a> are in most cases also included in the areas of the more widely recognised states that claim the same territory; see the notes in the "notes" column for each country for clarification.
</p><p>Not included in the list are <a href="/wiki/Territorial_claims_in_Antarctica" title="Territorial claims in Antarctica">individual country claims</a> to parts of the continent of <a href="/wiki/Antarctica" title="Antarctica">Antarctica</a> or entities such as the <a href="/wiki/European_Union" title="European Union">European Union</a><sup id="cite_ref-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-3">&#91;Note 2&#93;</a></sup> that have some degree of sovereignty but do not consider themselves to be sovereign countries or dependent territories.
</p><p>This list includes three measurements of area:
</p>
<ul><li><b>Total area:</b> the sum of land and water areas within international boundaries and coastlines.</li>
<li><b>Land area:</b> the aggregate of all land within international boundaries and coastlines, excluding water area.</li>
<li><b>Water area:</b> the sum of the surface areas of all inland water bodies (lakes, <a href="/wiki/Reservoir" title="Reservoir">reservoirs</a>, and rivers) within international boundaries and coastlines.<sup id="cite_ref-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-4">&#91;2&#93;</a></sup> Coastal <a href="/wiki/Internal_waters" title="Internal waters">internal waters</a> may be included. <a href="/wiki/Territorial_waters#Territorial_sea" title="Territorial waters">Territorial seas</a> are not included unless otherwise noted. Contiguous zones and <a href="/wiki/Exclusive_economic_zone" title="Exclusive economic zone">exclusive economic zones</a> are not included.</li></ul>
<p>Data is taken from the <a href="/wiki/United_Nations_Statistics_Division" title="United Nations Statistics Division">United Nations Statistics Division</a> unless otherwise noted.<sup id="cite_ref-unstats21_5-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-unstats21-5">&#91;3&#93;</a></sup>
</p>
<meta property="mw:PageProp/toc" />
<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Map">Map</span></h2>

<td><span data-sort-value="7014510072000000000♠"></span>510,072,000 (196,940,000)</td>

<tr>
<td><span data-sort-value="7000100000000000000♠">1</span></td>
<td style="text-align:left;"><span class="flagicon" style="display:inline-block;width:25px;"><img alt="" src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/f/f3/Flag_of_Russia.svg/23px-Flag_of_Russia.svg.png" decoding="async" width="23" height="15" class="thumbborder" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/f/f3/Flag_of_Russia.svg/35px-Flag_of_Russia.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/en/thumb/f/f3/Flag_of_Russia.svg/45px-Flag_of_Russia.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="900" data-file-height="600" /></span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Russia" title="Russia">Russia</a></td>
<td><span data-sort-value="7013170982460000000♠"></span>17,098,246 (6,601,670)</td>
<td><span data-sort-value="7013163784100000000♠"></span>16,378,410 (6,323,740)</td>
<td><span data-sort-value="7011719836000000000♠"></span>719,836 (277,930)</td>
<td>4.21</td>
<td style="text-align:left;">The largest country in the world, which spans <a href="/wiki/List_of_transcontinental_countries#Asia_and_Europe" title="List of transcontinental countries">two continents</a>, and most of northern <a href="/wiki/Eurasia" title="Eurasia">Eurasia</a>; with about 10.995% (about 1/9th) of the world's total landmass. Russia's <a href="/wiki/European_Russia" title="European Russia">European portion</a> is roughly 4,000,000 km<sup>2</sup>, which is around 40% of the total landmass of <a href="/wiki/Europe" title="Europe">Europe</a>, making Russia the <a href="/wiki/List_of_European_countries_by_area" title="List of European countries by area">largest country</a> in Europe; and its <a href="/wiki/Siberia" title="Siberia">Asian portion</a>, which covers all of <a href="/wiki/North_Asia" title="North Asia">Northern Asia</a>, is around 13,100,000 km<sup>2</sup>, making Russia the <a href="/wiki/List_of_Asian_countries_by_area" title="List of Asian countries by area">largest country</a> in <a href="/wiki/Asia" title="Asia">Asia</a>.
</td></tr>
<tr>
<td><span data-sort-value="1vend" style="display:none;"></span> –</td>

<td><span data-sort-value="7013142000000000000♠"></span>14,200,000 (5,500,000)</td>
<td><span data-sort-value="7013142000000000000♠"></span>14,200,000 (5,500,000)</td>
<td><span data-sort-value="5000000000000000000♠"></span>0 (0)</td>
<td>0</td>
<td style="text-align:left;">13,916,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (5,373,000&#160;sq&#160;mi) (98%) of the land area is covered by <a href="/wiki/Ice" title="Ice">ice</a> and <a href="/wiki/Snow" title="Snow">snow</a>. Though not itself a country, areas of Antarctica are <a href="/wiki/Territorial_claims_in_Antarctica" title="Territorial claims in Antarctica">claimed by a number of countries</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-6">&#91;Note 3&#93;</a></sup>
</td></tr>

<td style="text-align:left;">Largest <a href="/wiki/English_language" title="English language">English</a>- and largest <a href="/wiki/French_language" title="French language">French</a>-speaking country; largest country completely in the <a href="/wiki/Western_Hemisphere" title="Western Hemisphere">Western Hemisphere</a> by total area (second-largest by land area after the <a href="/wiki/United_States" title="United States">United States</a>); with the largest surface area of water; largest contiguous country in North America; Total area and water area figures include area covered by freshwater only and do not include internal waters (non-freshwater) of about 1,600,000 km<sup>2</sup>, nor territorial waters of 200,000 km<sup>2</sup>.<sup id="cite_ref-7" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-7">&#91;4&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-8" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-8">&#91;5&#93;</a></sup>

<td style="text-align:left;"><a href="/wiki/Territorial_dispute" title="Territorial dispute">Disputed territory</a> with <a href="/wiki/Political_status_of_Western_Sahara" title="Political status of Western Sahara">undetermined political status</a>. Administration is split between <a href="/wiki/Morocco" title="Morocco">Morocco</a> and the <a href="/wiki/Sahrawi_Arab_Democratic_Republic" title="Sahrawi Arab Democratic Republic">Sahrawi Arab Democratic Republic</a>, both of which claim the <a href="/wiki/Western_Sahara" title="Western Sahara">entire territory</a>. The Sahrawi Arab Democratic Republic's administrative control is limited to approximately <a href="/wiki/Free_Zone_(region)" title="Free Zone (region)">20% of the territory</a>, with the remaining <a href="/wiki/Southern_Provinces" title="Southern Provinces">80% of the territory</a> occupied by Morocco (see <a href="/wiki/Western_Sahara_conflict" title="Western Sahara conflict">Western Sahara conflict</a>).

Having unilaterally declared independence from <a href="/wiki/Serbia" title="Serbia">Serbia</a> in 2008, Kosovo <a href="/wiki/International_recognition_of_Kosovo" title="International recognition of Kosovo">is formally recognised as an independent state</a> by 101 out of 193 (<span data-sort-value="7001523316062176165♠" style="display:none"></span>52.3%) UN member states (with another 13 recognising it at some point but then withdrawing their recognition), while Serbia continues to claim it as part of its own territory.

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Charts">Charts</span></h2>
<p>The charts below are based on the <i><a href="/wiki/The_World_Factbook" title="The World Factbook">CIA World Factbook</a></i><sup id="cite_ref-109" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-109">&#91;43&#93;</a></sup> as of 15 February 2005.
</p><p><a href="/wiki/Sovereign_state" title="Sovereign state">Sovereign states</a> with areas greater than 100,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> are shown in green. In addition, non-sovereign territories are included for purposes of comparison, and are shown in gray. Areas include inland water bodies (lakes, reservoirs, rivers). Claims to parts of <a href="/wiki/Antarctica" title="Antarctica">Antarctica</a> by various countries are not included.
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Explanatory_notes">Explanatory notes</span></h2>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1011085734">.mw-parser-output .reflist{font-size:90%;margin-bottom:0.5em;list-style-type:decimal}.mw-parser-output .reflist .references{font-size:100%;margin-bottom:0;list-style-type:inherit}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-2{column-width:30em}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns-3{column-width:25em}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns{margin-top:0.3em}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns ol{margin-top:0}.mw-parser-output .reflist-columns li{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid-column}.mw-parser-output .reflist-upper-alpha{list-style-type:upper-alpha}.mw-parser-output .reflist-upper-roman{list-style-type:upper-roman}.mw-parser-output .reflist-lower-alpha{list-style-type:lower-alpha}.mw-parser-output .reflist-lower-greek{list-style-type:lower-greek}.mw-parser-output .reflist-lower-roman{list-style-type:lower-roman}</style><div class="reflist reflist-columns references-column-width" style="column-width: 30em;">
<ol class="references">
<li id="cite_note-Canada-1"><span class="mw-cite-backlink">^ <a href="#cite_ref-Canada_1-0"><sup><i><b>a</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-Canada_1-1"><sup><i><b>b</b></i></sup></a></span> <span class="reference-text">With its surface area including all or parts of 17 of the world's 40 largest freshwater lakes, Canada is smaller than China and the United States in land area.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-3"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-3">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The <a href="/wiki/European_Union" title="European Union">European Union</a> is a unique <a href="/wiki/Supranational_union" title="Supranational union">supranational union</a>. It covers a total area of 4,236,351&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (1,635,664&#160;sq&#160;mi),<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> and would be ranked 7th if it were included (3.0% of world's total land area).</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-6"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-6">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The following countries have <a href="/wiki/Territorial_claims_in_Antarctica" title="Territorial claims in Antarctica">territorial claims in Antarctica</a>: <a href="/wiki/Argentine_Antarctica" title="Argentine Antarctica">Argentina</a> (969,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 374,000&#160;sq&#160;mi), <a href="/wiki/Australian_Antarctic_Territory" title="Australian Antarctic Territory">Australia</a> (5,896,500&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 2,276,700&#160;sq&#160;mi), <a href="/wiki/Ant%C3%A1rtica_Chilena_Province" title="Antártica Chilena Province">Chile</a> (1,250,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 480,000&#160;sq&#160;mi), <a href="/wiki/Ad%C3%A9lie_Land" title="Adélie Land">France</a> (432,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 167,000&#160;sq&#160;mi), <a href="/wiki/Ross_Dependency" title="Ross Dependency">New Zealand</a> (450,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 170,000&#160;sq&#160;mi), <a href="/wiki/Queen_Maud_Land" title="Queen Maud Land">Norway</a> (including <a href="/wiki/Peter_I_Island" title="Peter I Island">Peter I Island</a>) (2,500,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 970,000&#160;sq&#160;mi) and <a href="/wiki/British_Antarctic_Territory" title="British Antarctic Territory">United Kingdom</a> (1,395,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 539,000&#160;sq&#160;mi), which to some extent overlap. The area from <span class="plainlinksneverexpand"><a class="external text" href="https://geohack.toolforge.org/geohack.php?pagename=List_of_countries_and_dependencies_by_area&amp;params=60_0_S_90_0_W_">90°W</a></span> to <span class="plainlinksneverexpand"><a class="external text" href="https://geohack.toolforge.org/geohack.php?pagename=List_of_countries_and_dependencies_by_area&amp;params=60_0_S_150_0_W_">150°W</a></span> (2,100,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 810,000&#160;sq&#160;mi) is currently unclaimed territory. These areas usually have no human inhabitants, excluding scientists.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-3rd-largest-10"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-3rd-largest_10-0">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The <a href="/wiki/United_Nations_Statistics_Division" title="United Nations Statistics Division">United Nations Statistics Division</a>, the <a href="/wiki/The_World_Factbook" title="The World Factbook">CIA World Factbook</a>, and the <a href="/wiki/Encyclop%C3%A6dia_Britannica" title="Encyclopædia Britannica">Encyclopædia Britannica</a> all rank the United States ahead of China in total area.  However, the figures used by each source <i>include</i> coastal and territorial waters for the United States but <i>exclude</i> coastal and territorial waters for China.

China's coastal and territorial water figures are currently unknown (no official publication) and thus cannot be added into China's total area figure.  But notably, Encyclopædia Britannica specifies the United States' area excluding coastal and territorial waters as 9,525,067 square kilometres (3,677,649&#160;sq&#160;mi),<sup id="cite_ref-United_States_9-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-United_States-9">&#91;6&#93;</a></sup> less than the figure given for China's area by any of the three sources.  Therefore, while it can be determined that China has a larger area excluding coastal and territorial waters, it is unclear which country has a larger area including coastal and territorial waters.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-12"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-12">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Approx. 7% of Brazil's land is located in the Northern Hemisphere. Its Southern Hemisphere portion (approx. 7,920,000 km<sup>2</sup>) is still significantly bigger than Australia. Although the U.S. has <a href="/wiki/American_Samoa" title="American Samoa">American Samoa</a>, <a href="/wiki/Jarvis_Island" title="Jarvis Island">Jarvis Island</a>, and a small portion of its <a href="/wiki/Territorial_waters#Territorial_sea" title="Territorial waters">territorial sea</a> south of <a href="/wiki/Baker_Island" title="Baker Island">Baker Island</a> in the Southern Hemisphere, it is not generally regarded as a Southern Hemisphere country.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-17"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-17">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Figures exclude the external territories of <a href="/wiki/Norfolk_Island" title="Norfolk Island">Norfolk Island</a>, <a href="/wiki/Ashmore_and_Cartier_Islands" title="Ashmore and Cartier Islands">Ashmore and Cartier Islands</a>, <a href="/wiki/Christmas_Island" title="Christmas Island">Christmas Island</a>, <a href="/wiki/Cocos_(Keeling)_Islands" title="Cocos (Keeling) Islands">Cocos (Keeling) Islands</a>, <a href="/wiki/Coral_Sea_Islands_Territory" class="mw-redirect" title="Coral Sea Islands Territory">Coral Sea Islands Territory</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Heard_Island_and_McDonald_Islands" title="Heard Island and McDonald Islands">Heard Island and McDonald Islands</a>,<sup id="cite_ref-14" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-14">&#91;9&#93;</a></sup> each shown separately. Excludes the claim of the <a href="/wiki/Australian_Antarctic_Territory" title="Australian Antarctic Territory">Australian Antarctic Territory</a> (5,896,500&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 2,276,700&#160;sq&#160;mi). The UN's previous data,<sup id="cite_ref-unstats07_15-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-unstats07-15">&#91;10&#93;</a></sup> as well as the current data shown by the World Bank and the CIA,<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-wbstats_16-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-wbstats-16">&#91;11&#93;</a></sup> give the figure as 7,741,220&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (2,988,900&#160;sq&#160;mi).</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-19"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-19">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The total area given is from <i><a href="/wiki/Encyclop%C3%A6dia_Britannica" title="Encyclopædia Britannica">Encyclopædia Britannica</a></i> and includes <a href="/wiki/List_of_territorial_disputes#Asia" title="List of territorial disputes">disputed territories</a> not under Indian control but claimed by India. The total area claimed by India, according to the figure given by the United Nations and the Indian government, totals 3,287,263&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (1,269,219&#160;sq&#160;mi).<sup id="cite_ref-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-18">&#91;12&#93;</a></sup></span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-20"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-20">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Excludes claims on <a href="/wiki/Argentine_Antarctica" title="Argentine Antarctica">Argentine Antarctica</a> (969,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 374,000&#160;sq&#160;mi), on <a href="/wiki/Falkland_Islands" title="Falkland Islands">Malvinas/Falkland Islands</a> (12,173&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 4,700&#160;sq&#160;mi) and on <a href="/wiki/South_Georgia_and_the_South_Sandwich_Islands" title="South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands">South Georgia and the South Sandwich Islands</a> (3,903&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 1,507&#160;sq&#160;mi).</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-23"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-23">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The area of internal waters is officially reported as 0<sup id="cite_ref-22" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-22">&#91;14&#93;</a></sup> even though several <a href="/wiki/Chott" title="Chott">seasonal lakes</a> and permanent <a href="/wiki/List_of_dams_and_reservoirs#Algeria" title="List of dams and reservoirs">reservoirs</a> exist in the country.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-24"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-24">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">A total of 1,755,636&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (677,855&#160;sq&#160;mi) (81%) of the land area is covered by <a href="/wiki/Ice" title="Ice">ice</a>; the ice-free area amounts to 410,450&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (158,480&#160;sq&#160;mi).<sup id="cite_ref-wbstats_16-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-wbstats-16">&#91;11&#93;</a></sup></span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-Sudan-26"><span class="mw-cite-backlink">^ <a href="#cite_ref-Sudan_26-0"><sup><i><b>a</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-Sudan_26-1"><sup><i><b>b</b></i></sup></a></span> <span class="reference-text">The water area percentage of Sudan prior to the secession of <a href="/wiki/South_Sudan" title="South Sudan">South Sudan</a> was 5.18.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-28"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-28">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The area given is from the 2007 data and the CIA World Factbook.<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-3" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup><sup id="cite_ref-unstats07_15-1" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-unstats07-15">&#91;10&#93;</a></sup> The <a href="/wiki/Yearbook_of_the_United_Nations" title="Yearbook of the United Nations">UN Demographic Yearbook</a> lists a figure of 1,628,750&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (628,860&#160;sq&#160;mi), which refers to land area only.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-29"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-29">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Excluding the <a href="/wiki/Ilemi_Triangle" title="Ilemi Triangle">Ilemi Triangle</a> (14,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 5,400&#160;sq&#160;mi).</span>

<li id="cite_note-30"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-30">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Excludes claims on <a href="/wiki/Guayana_Esequiba" title="Guayana Esequiba">Guayana Esequiba</a> (160,000&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 62,000&#160;sq&#160;mi)</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-32"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-32">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">By default, internationally sourced and published statistics include data for the Pakistani-administered territories of <a href="/wiki/Kashmir" title="Kashmir">Kashmir</a>: <a href="/wiki/Azad_Kashmir" title="Azad Kashmir">Azad Kashmir</a> (13,297&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 5,134&#160;sq&#160;mi) and <a href="/wiki/Gilgit-Baltistan" title="Gilgit-Baltistan">Gilgit-Baltistan</a> (72,971&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 28,174&#160;sq&#160;mi) in this area value.<sup id="cite_ref-31" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-31">&#91;17&#93;</a></sup> Further including <a href="/wiki/Kashmir_conflict" title="Kashmir conflict">Pakistani claims</a> over the Indian-administered territories of <a href="/wiki/Jammu_and_Kashmir_(union_territory)" title="Jammu and Kashmir (union territory)">Jammu and Kashmir</a> (42,241&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 16,309&#160;sq&#160;mi) and <a href="/wiki/Ladakh" title="Ladakh">Ladakh</a> (59,146&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 22,836&#160;sq&#160;mi) would produce a total land area figure of 984,010 km<sup>2</sup> (379,928 sq mi), and a total area figure of 1,009,230 km<sup>2</sup> (389,666 sq mi) for the country. Excluding all of these territories in the disputed Kashmir region (including Pakistani-administered territories) would produce a land area figure of 796,355 km<sup>2</sup> (307,473 sq mi) and a total area figure of 821,575 km<sup>2</sup> (317,212 sq mi). The total area figure is equal to Pakistan's land area added to its water area of 25,220 km<sup>2</sup> (9,740 sq mi).</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-33"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-33">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Excludes <a href="/wiki/Ant%C3%A1rtica_Chilena_Province" title="Antártica Chilena Province">claims in Antarctica</a>.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-ciadata-34"><span class="mw-cite-backlink">^ <a href="#cite_ref-ciadata_34-0"><sup><i><b>a</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-ciadata_34-1"><sup><i><b>b</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-ciadata_34-2"><sup><i><b>c</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-ciadata_34-3"><sup><i><b>d</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-ciadata_34-4"><sup><i><b>e</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-ciadata_34-5"><sup><i><b>f</b></i></sup></a></span> <span class="reference-text">Figures shown in this entry are taken from the CIA.<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-4" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup></span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-37"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-37">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Source gives area of <a href="/wiki/Metropolitan_France" title="Metropolitan France">metropolitan France</a> as 551,500&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (212,900&#160;sq&#160;mi) and lists overseas regions separately, whose areas sum to 89,179&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (34,432&#160;sq&#160;mi). Adding these give the total shown here for the entire <a href="/wiki/French_Fifth_Republic" title="French Fifth Republic">French Republic</a>. The CIA reports the total as 643,801&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (248,573&#160;sq&#160;mi).<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-5" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> Another source, the French <a href="/wiki/Institut_national_de_la_statistique_et_des_%C3%A9tudes_%C3%A9conomiques" title="Institut national de la statistique et des études économiques">Institut national de la statistique et des études économiques</a> (National Institute for Statistics and Economic Studies), gives an area figure of 632,759&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (244,310&#160;sq&#160;mi).<sup id="cite_ref-INSEE_36-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-INSEE-36">&#91;19&#93;</a></sup> (The CIA gives a water area of metropolitan France as 1,530&#160;km<sup>2</sup> (590&#160;sq&#160;mi).)</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-38"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-38">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">Includes the <a href="/wiki/Russian-occupied_territories_of_Ukraine" title="Russian-occupied territories of Ukraine">Russian-occupied territories of Ukraine</a></span>

<li id="cite_note-101"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-101">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The Cook Islands, in <a href="/wiki/Associated_state" title="Associated state">free association</a> with <a href="/wiki/New_Zealand" title="New Zealand">New Zealand</a>, has <a href="/wiki/Foreign_relations_of_the_Cook_Islands" title="Foreign relations of the Cook Islands">limited international recognition</a></span>

<li id="cite_note-2007data-102"><span class="mw-cite-backlink">^ <a href="#cite_ref-2007data_102-0"><sup><i><b>a</b></i></sup></a> <a href="#cite_ref-2007data_102-1"><sup><i><b>b</b></i></sup></a></span> <span class="reference-text">Data is from the 2007 statistics.<sup id="cite_ref-unstats07_15-6" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-unstats07-15">&#91;10&#93;</a></sup></span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-103"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-103">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The UN gives no information for the British Indian Ocean Territory. The figure given here was taken from the CIA Factbook, and refers to land area only.<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-17" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup></span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-104"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-104">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The figure shown is from the CIA Factbook, and includes <a href="/wiki/Henderson_Island_(Pitcairn_Islands)" title="Henderson Island (Pitcairn Islands)">Henderson</a>, <a href="/wiki/Ducie_Island" title="Ducie Island">Ducie</a>, and <a href="/wiki/Oeno_Island" title="Oeno Island">Oeno Island</a>.<sup id="cite_ref-ciastats_2-18" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-ciastats-2">&#91;1&#93;</a></sup> The UN figure is 5&#160;km<sup>2</sup> or 1.9&#160;sq&#160;mi, and only includes area for Pitcairn island itself, the only inhabited island in the group.</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-105"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-105">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text"><span class="url"><a rel="nofollow" class="external text" href="http://www.dscc.gov.mo/ENG/knowledge/geo_statistic.html">Current statistics: March 2014 – Direcção dos Serviços de Cartografia e Cadastro (Cartography and Cadastre Bureau)</a></span> Including <a href="/wiki/University_of_Macau" title="University of Macau">University of Macau</a>'s New <a href="/wiki/Hengqin" title="Hengqin">Hengqin</a> Campus</span>
</li>
<li id="cite_note-108"><span class="mw-cite-backlink"><b><a href="#cite_ref-108">^</a></b></span> <span class="reference-text">The <a href="https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Calendario_Atlante_De_Agostini" class="extiw" title="it:Calendario Atlante De Agostini">De Agostini Atlas Calendar</a> listed the area of Vatican City as 0.44&#160;km<sup>2</sup> in its 1930 edition<sup id="cite_ref-106" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-106">&#91;41&#93;</a></sup> but corrected it to 0.49&#160;km<sup>2</sup> in its 1945–46 edition.<sup id="cite_ref-agostini1945_107-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-agostini1945-107">&#91;42&#93;</a></sup> The figure of 0.44&#160;km<sup>2</sup> is still widely cited by many sources despite its inaccuracy.</span>
</li>
</ol></div>
